Conclusion: 当前水印方案在面对多模型访问场景时存在根本性脆弱性，要实现鲁棒的AI文本检测要么接受这一漏洞存在，要么要求模型提供商之间进行前所未有的协调。

Abstract: Watermarking embeds statistical signatures in AI-generated text for detection and attribution. We reveal a fundamental vulnerability: when users access multiple models (today's reality), watermarks trivially fail. Watermarks perturb output distributions away from the original, and in competitive markets, these perturbations are typically independent across providers. We theoretically prove that averaging output probability distributions recovers the unwatermarked distribution with up to a second-order error term. Empirically, simply averaging 3-5 models cancels out these perturbations. We introduce WASH (Watermark Attenuation via Statistical Hybridisation), which solves practical challenges in ensemble generation: vocabulary misalignment and tokenisation differences across heterogeneous models. Experiments across six watermarking schemes and three LLMs show that averaging across 3 models suppresses detection z-scores from 5-300 to below 2 (below the detection threshold of 4) and reduces TPR at 5% FPR to below 50%, while improving quality by 27.5% and running 6 times faster than the best baseline on the long sequence generation. Our results suggest that robust AI-text detection via watermarking requires either accepting this fundamental vulnerability or unprecedented coordination among model providers.

</details>


### [115] [Auditing LLM Benchmarks with Item Response Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30504)
*Sander Land,Daniel M. Bikel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM基准测试标签存在错误，并利用项目反应理论（IRT）识别出高精度错误标签，揭示了标签错误的来源（机械启发式、上游标注错误、歧义项），同时发现奖励模型存在风格偏好而非事实性知识的问题，以及前沿模型可能存在污染或过度优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试的标签在发布后便冻结不变，但其中存在错误，这些错误会无声地传播到下游基准测试中，影响评估结果的可靠性。

Method: 基于项目反应理论（IRT）构建指标，使用114个模型在7个偏好与多项选择基准测试上的响应，在top 200样本中以95%精度识别疑似错误标签，并与有监督分类器进行对比。

Result: 所提IRT指标在top 200样本中达到95%精度，优于有监督分类器；发现标签错误来源于机械标注启发式、上游数据集继承的错误，以及无法给出唯一正确标签的歧义项；奖励模型更擅长区分风格偏好而非事实性知识；某一前沿奖励模型与检测到的错误标签的一致性高达78%，而同类模型仅为38%。

Conclusion: 基准测试标签错误普遍存在且可被系统性地检测，亟需更严谨的标注流程；奖励模型存在偏见，前沿模型可能受到基准污染或过度优化的影响。

Abstract: LLM benchmark labels are frozen at release and silently propagated into downstream benchmarks, errors and all. We introduce an Item Response Theory-based indicator that surfaces likely mislabels at 95% precision in the top 200 examples across seven preference and multiple-choice benchmarks using responses from 114 models, outperforming a supervised classifier. We trace these errors to mechanical labeling heuristics, upstream annotation mistakes inherited unchanged from source datasets, and fundamentally ambiguous items without a defensible single label. The same model fit reveals that reward models specialize in stylistic preference rather than factual knowledge, and identifies one frontier reward model that agrees with detected mislabels at 78% accuracy versus 38% for its peers, consistent with benchmark contamination or benchmark-specific over-optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Generalistic or Specific Embeddings, Which is Better? An Empirical Study on Search for Clinical Coding in Non-English Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30529)
*David Rey-Blanco,Roberto Cruz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用大语言模型生成合成数据（涵盖六种语言）来微调西班牙语生物医学编码器，构建两阶段检索器（Bi-Encoder + Cross-Encoder Reranker），在临床ICD编码检索任务上显著提升了多语言召回率，整体MRR提升15.9%，且跨语言性能优于BioBERT-ST基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有句子嵌入模型在非英语临床检索（尤其是ICD-10-CM/CIE-10编码检索）中表现不佳，且聚合基准掩盖了各语言的性能退化。作者希望利用大语言模型的生成能力来弥补这一跨语言临床检索差距。

Method: 使用Gemini生成覆盖英语、西班牙语、加泰罗尼亚语、意大利语、葡萄牙语和法语的合成训练数据（约19,500对），对西班牙语生物医学编码器PlanTL-GOB-ES/bsc-bio-ehr-es进行微调，构建Bi-Encoder + Cross-Encoder Reranker两阶段检索流水线。

Result: Bi-Encoder在MRR（0.876 vs 0.866）、R@3（0.650 vs 0.626）和R@5（0.804 vs 0.790）上超越BioBERT-ST；加入Cross-Encoder后R@5提升至0.822，在西班牙语（+0.017）、加泰罗尼亚语（+0.033）、法语（+0.018）、葡萄牙语（+0.037）上占优，仅英语有微小下降；葡萄牙语R@5达0.829（基线0.714）。

Conclusion: LLM生成数据可以作为有效的"数据工厂"构建领域特定多语言医疗检索器，临床可接受的跨语言性能提升验证了该方案的有效性；贡献包括开源配方、量化学习增益及语言与排名维度上的增益分布分析。

Abstract: Sentence-embedding models for semantic search are overwhelmingly developed and evaluated on English corpora. When applied to clinical retrieval in other languages -- particularly retrieval of ICD-10-CM / CIE-10 codes -- recall degrades in ways often masked by aggregate benchmarks. We study whether large generative language models can serve as data factories to close this gap. We build a two-stage retriever (bi-encoder followed by cross-encoder reranker), fine-tuned from a Spanish biomedical encoder (PlanTL-GOB-ES/bsc-bio-ehr-es) on Gemini-generated synthetic data covering English, Spanish, Catalan, Italian, Portuguese and French, and evaluate against BioBERT-ST and the un-tuned Spanish encoder. The bi-encoder alone matches BioBERT-ST on MRR (0.876 vs. 0.866) and overtakes it on R@3 (0.650 vs. 0.626) and R@5 (0.804 vs. 0.790) without English biomedical pretraining. Adding a cross-encoder reranker lifts aggregate R@5 to 0.822 and dominates on four of five languages (+0.017 Spanish, +0.033 Catalan, +0.018 French, +0.037 Portuguese) at the cost of a small English regression. The trade-off is clinically acceptable: Portuguese reaches R@5 = 0.829 vs. BioBERT-ST's 0.714. Contributions: an open recipe for building domain-specific medical retrievers from LLM-generated data; quantification of the learning gain (MRR 0.755 to 0.876, +15.9% with ~19,500 synthetic pairs); and a characterisation of where gains concentrate by language and rank.

</details>


### [117] [Refining Word-Based Grammatical Error Annotation for L2 Korean](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30545)
*Jungyeul Park,Kyungtae Lim,Wonjun Oh,Benjamin Nguyen,Zihao Huang,Mengyang Qiu,Jayoung Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对韩语语法纠错中词级评价与语素级错误之间的结构不匹配问题，重构了NIKL语料库的目标句，定义了韩语专用ERRANT式编辑标注方案，并引入多参考评估设置，实验验证了改进资源能有效提升纠错效果并减少单参考评估的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 韩语语法纠错存在词级评估与语素级错误之间的结构性不匹配，现有资源存在目标句实现、韩语特有编辑标注和单参考评估三个问题。

Method: 1) 基于形态约束规则重构NIKL语料库目标句；2) 将语素级标注转换为词级m2编辑格式；3) 定义韩语ERRANT式标注方案，区分功能语素错误、拼写错误、词界错误和词序错误；4) 为KoLLA语料库增加额外参考修正，构建多参考评估。

Result: 重构的NIKL目标句困惑度更低；转换的m2文件在源-目标编辑表示上一致性更高；改进资源在相同模型设置下提升了KoBART的纠错效果；多参考KoLLA评估减少了对合理修正的惩罚。

Conclusion: 韩语GEC评估不仅依赖纠错模型，还依赖于反映韩语形态、空格和修正变异性的参考数据与编辑标注。

Abstract: Korean grammatical error correction (K-GEC) presents a structural mismatch between word-based evaluation and the morpheme-level locus of many learner errors. Postpositions and verbal endings are bound to lexical hosts, but they encode grammatical relations that must be represented in correction and evaluation. This paper refines word-based grammatical error annotation for L2 Korean by addressing three connected problems in existing resources: surface target realization, Korean-specific edit annotation, and single-reference evaluation. We reconstruct target sentences from the National Institute of Korean Language (NIKL) L2 corpus under morphologically constrained realization rules and convert its morpheme-level annotations into word-level \texttt{m2} edits. We then define a Korean ERRANT-style annotation scheme that preserves the MRU core while distinguishing functional morpheme errors, spelling errors, word boundary errors, and word order errors. We also augment the KoLLA corpus with an additional reference correction, yielding a multi-reference evaluation setting for Korean GEC. Empirical validation shows that the refined NIKL targets yield lower perplexity, the converted \texttt{m2} files achieve higher agreement with source-target edit representations, and the refined resources improve KoBART-based correction under the same model setting. Multi-reference KoLLA evaluation further reduces the penalty imposed on valid corrections that diverge from a single reference, especially for neural and prompted GEC systems. These results show that Korean GEC evaluation depends not only on correction models, but also on reference data and edit annotations that reflect Korean morphology, spacing, and correction variability.

</details>


### [118] [Generating and Refining Dynamic Evaluation Rubrics for LLM-as-a-Judge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30568)
*Zijie Wang,Eduardo Blanco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出无需人工标注的自动生成细粒度评估标准方法，通过元评判奖励信号微调标准生成器，在成对和逐点评估中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标准的LLM评估方法依赖人工标注数据（如参考答案或专家制定的标准），成本高且可扩展性有限，因此需要自动化的标准生成方法。

Method: 1) 无训练方法：在数据集级别和实例级别自动生成细粒度评估标准；2) 微调方法：通过元评判奖励信号迭代微调标准生成器模型。

Result: 无训练方法在四个基准上达到与现有方法竞争的性能；微调后的14B标准生成器在成对和逐点评估中超越所有基线，甚至优于更大的专有模型。

Conclusion: 自动化标准生成无需人工标注即可实现高效评估，微调策略显著提升了标准生成器的性能，证明了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is a scalable alternative to human evaluation, yet existing rubric-based methods rely on human-annotated data such as reference answers or expert-crafted rubrics. We propose to automatically generate fine-grained evaluation rubrics without any human annotation. Our training-free method generates rubrics at dataset-specific and instance-specific granularities, achieving performance competitive with existing methods across four benchmarks. We further present a method that iteratively fine-tunes a rubric generator model via meta-judge reward signals. The fine-tuned generator outperforms all existing baselines in both pairwise and pointwise evaluation. Notably, a fine-tuned 14B rubric generator outperforms a much larger proprietary model at rubric generation, showing the effectiveness of our fine-tuning strategy.

</details>


### [119] [Probing the Prompt KV Cache: Where It Becomes Dispensable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30574)
*Vinayshekhar Bannihatti Kumar,Manoj Ghuhan Arivazhagan,Disha Makhija,Rashmi Gangadharaiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究发现，提示KV缓存的冗余主要源于聊天模板的形式（脚手架结构），而非实际内容。用中性填充词的模板KV缓存替换上层提示缓存可恢复准确率，而直接清零则导致性能崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示KV缓存在解码过程中的冗余本质：冗余发生在哪些层、多少步之后，以及以何种形式存在，从而理解何时以及何种提示KV缓存可以被替换而不影响任务性能。

Method: 通过可控拼接干预实验，扫描不同层截断点和解码步数，替换上层提示KV缓存为聊天模板脚手架（中性填充词）的KV缓存，与直接清零进行对比。

Result: 冗余在于聊天模板的形式（脚手架），而非内容。用模板脚手架替换可恢复接近完整准确率，而清零相同位置则准确率崩溃。该现象在Qwen3、Gemma 3、Llama 3系列及多个数据集上一致复现。

Conclusion: 提示KV缓存的冗余本质上是形式性（聊天模板结构）的冗余，可以利用这一特性进行更高效、更智能的缓存压缩。

Abstract: Prior KV cache compression schemes empirically demonstrate that the prompt cache is partially redundant during decoding, dropping or summarising entries with little accuracy loss. We ask when and what kind of redundancy: at which layers, after how many decoding steps, and in what form can the prompt span KV cache be replaced without breaking the task. A controlled splice intervention swept over layer cutoff and decoding steps shows this redundancy is about form (chat template scaffolding) rather than content. Replacing the upper layer prompt span KV cache with KV cache from a chat template scaffold whose user content is a neutral filler recovers near clean accuracy, while zeroing the same slots collapses accuracy. The dissociation replicates across the Qwen3, Gemma 3, and Llama 3 families on multiple datasets.

</details>


### [120] [Speculative Decoding Across Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30580)
*Nirajan Paudel,Michael Ginn,Luc De Nardi,Alexis Palmer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种策略（任务特定微调、无监督单语微调、n-gram草稿模型）来提升非英语语言中推测解码的效率，发现n-gram模型虽接受率低但因生成速度快而持续带来大幅加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小规模草稿模型在多语言能力上存在缺陷，导致在非英语语言文本生成时推测解码效率低下。

Method: 对比三种策略：1) 在翻译任务上微调草稿模型；2) 在无标注单语语料上微调草稿模型；3) 在相同单语语料上训练简单的n-gram草稿模型。在11种语言上评估翻译和故事生成任务。

Result: 任务特定蒸馏能显著提升效率，但泛化到新任务时效果不佳；n-gram草稿模型虽然接受率较低，但由于生成速度极快，仍能持续带来显著的加速效果。

Conclusion: n-gram草稿模型在多语言推测解码场景下是一种简单且高效的方案，尤其在跨任务泛化方面优于蒸馏模型。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a crucial component of large language model (LLM) inference, enabling faster generation by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel. However, small draft models tend to suffer from disproportionately poor multilingual capabilities. Thus, when generating text in a non-English language, speculative decoding is far less effective.
  We compare three strategies to improve speculative decoding efficiency for eleven languages: finetuning the draft model on task-specific data (translation); finetuning the draft model on unlabeled monolingual corpora; and training simple n-gram draft models on the same monolingual corpora. We evaluate efficiency on translation (from English into the target language) and the held-out task of story generation. We find that while task-specific distillation can significantly improve efficiency, distilled models generalize poorly to a new task. Meanwhile, n-gram draft models, despite lower acceptance rates, consistently provide large speed-ups due to much faster draft generation.

</details>


### [121] [AI for Monitoring and Classifying Data Used in Research Literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30582)
*Rafael Macalaba,Aivin V. Solatorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于GLiNER的多任务框架，结合合成数据生成和LLM重验证，实现研究文献中数据集使用情况的自动化监控与引用追踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏类似Google Scholar监控论文引用那样的基础设施来追踪数据集在研究文献中的使用情况，导致数据使用格局不透明，影响可重复性和影响力评估。

Method: 采用多任务GLiNER框架，联合执行数据集提及抽取、关系识别和使用上下文分类；利用合成数据生成解决标签稀缺问题，并结合LLM重验证过滤错误提及、保证标签一致性。

Result: 提升了数据集监控管道的可靠性、覆盖率和输出一致性，推动了可扩展的开源工具发展。

Conclusion: 本研究推进了通用、无约束的数据集引用追踪目标，为研究文献中数据使用监控提供开源解决方案。

Abstract: While platforms like Google Scholar and Semantic Scholar track citations for academic papers, no comparable infrastructure exists for monitoring dataset usage in research literature, leaving the landscape of data use largely opaque. Addressing this gap is critical for transparency, reproducibility, and monitoring of impact, yet progress is hindered by inconsistent citation practices, scarce labeled data, and ambiguous references to datasets in the wild. Traditional NLP approaches struggle with these challenges, motivating the shift toward more adaptive, semantically rich models. Building on prior work using LLMs for data mention detection and synthetic data for bootstrapping training, this paper presents an updated methodology for scalable dataset monitoring. We introduce a multitask GLiNER-based framework that jointly performs dataset mention extraction, relation identification, and usage-context classification. To address label scarcity, the pipeline leverages synthetic data generation to produce training examples and LLM-based revalidation to filter incorrect mentions and enforce labeling consistency, together improving reliability, coverage, and output consistency across the training pipeline. This work advances the development of open-source tools for monitoring data use in research literature, contributing to the broader goal of generalizable, unconstrained dataset citation tracking.

</details>


### [122] [Semantic Motion Anchors: Bridging Motion and Meaning in Co-Speech Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30608)
*Varsha Suresh,Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi,Mohamed Salman,M. Hamza Mughal,Christian Theobalt,Ashwin Ram,Jürgen Steimle,Vera Demberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义运动锚点（Semantic Motion Anchors）方法，通过将手势离散化为运动基元并转化为结构化文本描述，利用文本辅助对比学习，提升共语手势检索中语义对齐的质量，在BEAT2数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接在文本与连续运动嵌入之间进行对比对齐，往往过度关注低层运动学特征而忽略了手势的符号性语义内容，导致检索结果缺乏语义意义。

Method: 1) 将3D手势离散化为身体-手部运动基元；2) 将运动基元转化为结构化自然语言描述；3) 将这些语义运动锚点作为辅助对比监督信号，在文本与运动之间建立语义层面的对齐。

Result: 在BEAT2数据集上，文本到手势检索R@1提升8.2%；在文本到手势和手势到文本两个检索方向上均优于此前方法；用户偏好实验表明本文检索结果更符合作者意图。

Conclusion: 语义运动锚点监督能够帮助模型检索到与查询文本语义匹配的手势，而非默认的通用运动模式，语义对齐的检索结果能有效提升下游手势生成任务中沟通意图的表达。

Abstract: Learning a shared representation between spoken text and gesture is central to co-speech gesture retrieval, synthesis, and understanding, but remains challenging for semantically meaningful gestures whose communicative intent is not captured by motion alone. Direct contrastive alignment between transcripts and continuous motion embeddings often overemphasizes low-level kinematics and misses the symbolic content of semantic gestures. We propose semantic motion anchors, natural-language abstractions of gesture motion capturing physical form and communicative intent. Our method discretizes 3D gestures into body-hand motion primitives, verbalizes them into structured descriptions, and grounds them in the transcript to provide auxiliary contrastive supervision. On BEAT2, our method improves text-to-gesture R@1 by 8.2% over a direct text-motion baseline and outperforms prior retrieval approaches on text to gesture and gesture to text retrieval directions. Beyond aggregate retrieval metrics, semantic motion anchor supervision helps retrieve gestures that are semantically meaningful for the spoken query, rather than defaulting to generic motion patterns. A downstream retrieval-augmented gesture generation study showed that users significantly preferred gestures retrieved by our approach over a retrieval-augmented generation baseline, demonstrating that semantically grounded retrieval translates to gestures that better convey communicative intent in downstream generation.

</details>


### [123] [The Architecture of Errors: From Universal Impossibility to Patch-Local LLM Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30628)
*Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Lee Mosbacker,Sisong Bei,Ziwei Dong,Dmitri Kalaev,Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大语言模型可靠性在通用域中无界，但在操作有界补丁中失败模式稀疏可重复，可靠性变为局部目录发现与干预覆盖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法认为可靠性是无限库问题，无法保证对所有失败模式有界误差；但在实际部署中（法律审查、医疗RAG等），任务模式重复且失败集中，因此需要形式化从通用不可靠到局部可靠的理论转变。

Method: 提出两个命题和一个推论：命题1证明无限域下无有限干预字典能覆盖所有可区分失败模式；推论1给出模式发现的对数上界与硬失败事件的指数需求关系；命题2证明在局部补丁中，若失败模式集中且头部覆盖率充足，所需干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长，目录饱和后趋于常数。

Result: 理论框架将可靠性问题从指数级token长度问题重新定位为局部目录发现和干预覆盖问题；在操作有界补丁中，干预预算随序列长度呈多对数增长，目录饱和后为域常数。

Conclusion: 框架没有消解长上下文困难，而是将其重新定位：当硬决策数量随任务长度增长时，可靠性仍具挑战性；贡献在于将困难定位到正确的干预维度，而非使这些场景变得容易。

Abstract: Universal LLM reliability is not a finite-library problem: across all possible tasks, tools, schemas, knowledge sources, and evaluator expectations, new intervention-distinguishable failure modes can appear without bound, so no finite intervention dictionary can guarantee bounded residual error for every such mode. But deployed systems do not operate over the whole universe. They operate inside operationally bounded patches (legal review, medical RAG, code repair, customer-support agents, contract extraction) with recurring tasks, schemas, tools, and evaluator expectations. Within such patches, empirical evidence suggests failures are sparse, repetitive, and concentrated in a small recurring catalogue, so reliability becomes a local catalogue-discovery and intervention-coverage problem rather than an exponential token-length problem. We formalize this transition with two propositions and one corollary. Proposition 1 is the worst-case-mode-wise negative result: no finite intervention dictionary covers every distinguishable failure mode of an unbounded domain. Corollary 1 is the inverse-discovery implication: the logarithmic upper bound on mode discovery cannot accommodate linearly more distinct tail modes without exponentially more observed hard-failure events. Proposition 2 is the positive patch-local result: under log active-mode exposure and head-heavy coverage, a sufficient per-hard-decision intervention budget grows polylogarithmically in sequence length and becomes domain-constant once the patch catalogue saturates. The framework relocates rather than dissolves long-context difficulty: where the number of hard decisions itself grows with task length, reliability remains hard; the contribution is to identify the on-axis intervention rather than to make those regimes easy.

</details>


### [124] [COFT: Counterfactual-Conformal Decoding for Fair Chain-of-Thought Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30641)
*Arya Fayyazi,Mehdi Kamal,Massoud Pedram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: COFT是一种无需训练的推理时解码方法，通过对每个token进行公平性控制，在保证用户指定风险水平的前提下，将大语言模型链式思维生成中的社会偏见降低30-55%（中位数38%），且几乎不损失任务效用和语言质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在链式思维生成过程中会暴露并放大社会偏见，现有方法通常需要重训练、辅助分类器或权重访问，缺乏轻量、可审计、有统计保证的推理时偏见控制方案。

Method: COFT包含三个阶段：1) 通过掩码将敏感词替换为中性词构建反事实提示；2) 通过轻量级logit融合比较事实与掩码logit分布，削弱属性驱动偏见；3) 使用双分支分割保形校准，在用户指定风险水平下为每步候选token集提供分布自由边际有效性保证。

Result: 在六个模型和多个偏见基准上，标准偏见指标降低30-55%（中位数38%），推理准确率在运行波动范围内保持不变，计算开销仅相当于一次额外缓存前向传播（≤11%）。

Conclusion: COFT提供了一条清晰、可审计的路径，在显著减少偏见的同时几乎不损失效用，且无需重训练、辅助分类器或权重访问。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can reveal and amplify societal biases during chain-of-thought (CoT) generation. We present COFT (Chain of Fair Thought), a training-free decoding method that applies token-level fairness control at decode time, with distribution-free marginal validity guarantees (under exchangeability) for any frozen causal language model. COFT operates in three stages. First, it creates a masked counterfactual prompt by replacing sensitive spans with neutral tokens. Second, it compares the factual and masked logit distributions through lightweight logit fusion to attenuate attribute-driven biases. Third, it uses dual-branch split-conformal calibration to certify per-step candidate token sets at a user-chosen risk level. We evaluate COFT across six models and multiple bias benchmarks. Our method reduces standard bias metrics by 30-55% (median 38%) while preserving task utility and language quality. Reasoning accuracies remain unchanged within run-to-run noise margins. The computational overhead is modest, equivalent to one additional cached forward pass (<=11%). COFT offers a clear, auditable path to safer CoT generation with significant bias reduction, negligible utility loss, and no requirement for retraining, auxiliary classifiers, or weight access.

</details>


### [125] [Same Patient, Different Words, Different Diagnosis? Evaluating Semantic Stability in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30646)
*Mahdi Alkaeed,Adnan Qayyum,Nabeel Abo Kashreef,Muhammad Bilal,Junaid Qadir*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM在临床应用中因语言细微变化导致预测不一致的问题，提出基于自然语言推理（NLI）的语义验证框架，过滤保留语义的提示变体，并引入三个敏感性指标（MVS、ΔC、WCI）。实验表明，领域专用模型与通用模型的鲁棒性差异高度依赖具体模型，领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床应用中面对语义等价的输入变体时，预测结果高度敏感且不一致，而现有的嵌入相似度指标无法有效捕捉否定、时态、严重程度等关键语义差异，亟需一种能验证语义等价性的方法。

Method: 基于NLI的语义验证框架（过滤保留语义的提示变体） + LLM-as-a-judge精炼 + 临床专家审核；提出三个敏感性指标：MVS（语义保留变体敏感性）、ΔC（置信度变化）、WCI（最差情况不稳定性）。

Result: 在DiagnosisQA和MedQA数据集上评估16个通用和医学LLM，发现领域专用模型与通用模型的鲁棒性差异混合且高度依赖模型，领域专业化并不一致地提升或降低鲁棒性，部分领域模型最鲁棒，但强通用基线也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 领域专业化并不能保证对语义等价提示变体的鲁棒性，模型鲁棒性更多取决于具体模型架构与训练策略；临床应用中需对不同模型单独评估其提示敏感性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in clinical applications. However, their behavior remains highly sensitive to subtle linguistic variations, such as rephrasing or syntactic variation. This sensitivity poses risks in safety-critical healthcare settings, where semantically equivalent inputs should produce consistent predictions. However, a key challenge is to ensure that prompt variations truly preserve clinical meaning, as embedding-based similarity metrics often fail to capture distinctions involving negation, temporality, or severity. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic verification framework based on Natural Language Inference (NLI) to filter meaning-preserving prompt variations, which are further refined using an LLM-as-a-judge and audited by a clinical expert. In addition, we introduce three metrics to quantify model sensitivity: MeaningPreserving Variation Sensitivity (MVS), confidence variation (ΔC), and Worst-Case Instability (WCI). We evaluate 16 open-source general-purpose (GP) and medical LLMs within the same model families and parameter scales, using reformulated prompts derived from the DiagnosisQA and MedQA datasets. Our results demonstrate that robustness differences between domain-specific (DS) models are mixed and highly model-dependent, i.e., domain specialization does not consistently improve or reduce robustness to meaning-preserving prompt reformulations. Several DS models rank among the most robust (when compared with GP counterparts), and strong GP baselines remain competitive as well.

</details>


### [126] [Counterfactual Graph for Multi-Agent LLM Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30653)
*Jiatan Huang,Mingchen Li,Ziming Li,Sunjae Kwon,Hong Yu,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CAGE-CAL框架，通过对通信后的智能体图与反事实无通信图进行对比校准，解决多智能体LLM系统中通信导致的一致性问题，在五个基准上提升可靠性判别和拓扑选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常将"多数同意"视为可靠证据，但智能体间通信会导致相关性失败和虚假共识，使得相同投票率在不同拓扑结构下反映不同的可靠性。

Method: 提出CAGE-CAL（反事实智能体图校准框架）：对比观测到的通信后智能体图与匹配的反事实无通信图，捕获成对失败相关性和组级依赖，估计反事实偏移并校准置信度。

Result: 在五个基准上，CAGE-CAL改善了可靠性判别（具有竞争性的ECE），且校准后的置信度在拓扑选择上优于最佳固定拓扑策略。

Conclusion: 通过反事实对比校准，能有效消除通信带来的虚假共识偏差，提升多智能体系统置信度校准的可靠性。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems often treat agreement as evidence: when many agents in a panel give the same answer, that answer is assumed to be more reliable. We show that this assumption can fail after agents communicate. Communication can induce correlated failures and false consensus, so the same vote share may reflect reliable agreement in one topology but over-confidence in another. We propose CAGE-CAL, a counterfactual agent-graph calibration framework for multi-agent LLMs. For each query, CAGE-CAL compares an observed post-communication agent graph with a matched counterfactual no-communication graph, capturing both pairwise failure correlations and group-level dependencies. Rather than simply counting how many agents agree, CAGE-CAL estimates the counterfactual shift between observed and no-communication dependence, and calibrates confidence accordingly. Across five benchmarks, CAGE-CAL improves reliability discrimination with competitive ECE, and its calibrated confidence further improves topology selection over the best fixed-topology strategy.

</details>


### [127] [EUDAIMONIA: Evaluating Undesirable Dynamics in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30654)
*Jun Rui Huang,Wang Bill Zhu,Ziyi Liu,Nathanael Fast,Ravi Iyer,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Social AI Design Code框架和EUDAIMONIA基准，评估LLM在社交互动中的用户福祉对齐问题，发现最强模型仍有30%以上违反率，且扩展思考无法解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为社交伴侣使用时，存在有害亲密、依赖、过度参与等风险，而传统安全评估未能覆盖这些社交动态带来的伤害。

Method: 构建Social AI Design Code框架，并基于WildChat通过弱到强过滤、多模型重标注、受控改写生成EUDAIMONIA基准（969条用户输入，3147项检查），评估22个LLM。

Result: 最强模型Claude-Opus-4.7违反30.7%检查，GPT-5.5违反27.2%检查；扩展思考不能降低违反率。

Conclusion: 社交对齐问题是持续性的对齐问题，而非单纯通过测试时推理可解决的缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as conversational partners for companionship, emotional disclosure, and interpersonal advice, but the social dynamics of these interactions can create harms that are not captured by capability-oriented or traditional safety evaluations. We introduce the Social AI Design Code, a framework for evaluating whether LLMs align with user welfare in social interactions, including whether they encourage harmful intimacy, dependence, or prolonged engagement. To evaluate these risks in natural and diverse user-LLM interactions, we operationalize the code with EUDAIMONIA, a benchmark of 969 user inputs and 3,147 design-requirement violation checks built from WildChat through weak-to-strong filtration, multi-model relabeling, and controlled rewriting. Evaluating 22 recent LLMs, we find that even the strongest models, Claude-Opus-4.7 and GPT-5.5, violate 30.7% and 27.2% of checks, respectively. Extended thinking does not reduce violation rates, suggesting that these failures are persistent social-alignment problems rather than deficits solvable through test-time reasoning alone.

</details>


### [128] [CobSeg: Coherence Boundary Modeling for Dialogue Topic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30668)
*Sijin Sun,Liangbin Zhao,Jiaxiang Cai,Ming Deng,Mingyu Luo,Xiuju Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CobSeg是一种新型多分支对话主题分割架构，通过分离语义连续性与词汇边界转换、利用边界信息加权和语料主题连贯性线索，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有非LLM方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有话语模型常稀释局部词汇信号，导致对话主题分割中边界线索（词汇转换与语义不连续性）识别不准确，亟需一种能同时捕捉两类线索的轻量级分割方法。

Method: 提出CobSeg多分支架构：1) 分离连贯性语义连续性与词汇边界转换；2) 通过方向性边界预测恢复两者；3) 引入边界信息加权强调高效用位置；4) 融入语料主题连贯性线索及学习组合权重。支持监督式与伪标签两种训练模式，推理时不依赖LLM。

Result: 在五个基准测试中表现优异：监督设置下VHF上P_k降低0.7、W_d降低0.6，DialSeg711上P_k达1.0；伪标签设置下VHF上P_k降低14.8，DialSeg711降低1.5，TIAGE降低1.1，全面超越非LLM方法。

Conclusion: CobSeg有效分离并融合了对话主题分割中的两类边界线索，以紧凑架构实现高性能，特别擅长利用局部词汇线索，为非LLM对话分割方法设定了新标杆。

Abstract: Dialogue topic segmentation is critical in many human-AI collaborative applications which requires identifying heterogeneous boundary cues, including lexical transitions near utterance edges and semantic discontinuities across utterances. Existing utterance models often dilute these local lexical signals. We propose CobSeg, a novel multi-branch architecture that separates coherence-level semantic continuity from lexical boundary transitions and recovers both through directional boundary prediction. CobSeg further uses boundary informativeness weighting to emphasize high-utility utterance positions, and incorporates a corpus-derived topic coherence cue with learned combination weights. While CobSeg is evaluated as a compact trainable segmenter under supervised gold-boundary training and a pseudo-label setting with automatically induced boundaries, it performs enhanced boundary prediction without LLM calls during inference. Across five benchmarks, it improves $P_k$ and $W_d$ particularly when local lexical cues are prominent: under gold supervision, it reduces $P_k$ by 0.7 points and $W_d$ by 0.6 points on VHF, and reaches $P_k$ of 1.0 on DialSeg711; with induced boundaries, it reduces $P_k$ by 14.8 points on VHF, by 1.5 points on DialSeg711, and by 1.1 points on TIAGE, outperforming prior non-LLM approaches.

</details>


### [129] [TeachObs: A Human-Validated Benchmark for Multimodal Teaching Observation and Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30673)
*Yeil Jeong,Youngjin Yoo,Seobin Sohn,Hyejin Han,Jinseo Lee,Scott Howard,Unggi Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeachObs，一个经过人工验证的课堂视频多模态教学观察基准，包含来自8个国家的30节公开课、5158个15秒场景和39种二元观察编码，并评估了五种前沿多模态大语言模型的表现，发现没有单一模型在所有任务上一致最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 课堂视频中包含可观察的教学实践，但其教学和视觉信号很少以适合模型评估的形式组织，因此需要构建一个标准化的基准来评估AI系统在课堂视频分析中的能力。

Method: 收集30节公开课视频（8个国家），切分为5158个15秒场景；7位研究者对每个场景标注39个二元编码（20个视觉编码+19个非视觉编码）；基于Krippendorff's alpha构建可靠性和流行度感知规则生成黄金标签；3位专家对30节课进行课程级别评分；评估五个多模态大语言模型在三种任务上的表现。

Result: 没有单一模型在所有三个任务上一致最优；添加中间帧会同时增加正确和错误的归因；模型评价相较于专家评价会高估程序清晰的课程。

Conclusion: TeachObs支持细粒度标注基准测试和整课评估，揭示了AI系统在课堂视频分析中可以辅助的方面以及仍需专家判断的方面。

Abstract: Classroom videos contain observable teaching practices, but their pedagogical and visual signals are rarely organized in forms suitable for model evaluation. We present \textit{TeachObs}, a human-validated benchmark for multimodal teaching observation in classroom videos. \textit{TeachObs} includes 30 public lesson videos from eight countries divided into 5,158 fixed 15-second scenes. Seven researchers annotated each scene with 39 binary observation codes, covering 20 visual codes, such as gesture, board work, pointing, and visual materials, and 19 nonvisual codes, such as instruction, monitoring, questioning, feedback, and reflection. Gold segment labels are constructed using reliability- and prevalence-aware rules based on Krippendorff's alpha. In addition to segment-level labels, three expert raters produced lesson-level ratings and qualitative evaluations of instructional design, instructional delivery, learner response, learning materials, and lesson closure across the 30 lessons, with rater coverage detailed in the body. Using these two human reference layers, we evaluate five vision-capable frontier LLMs across three tracks - text-only segment coding, text + frame segment coding, and lesson-level coverage scored under an LLM-as-judge protocol - and find that no single model consistently outperforms others across all three tracks, that adding a mid-frame inflates both true and false attributions per scene, and that model evaluations over-rate procedurally clear lessons relative to expert raters. \textit{TeachObs} therefore supports both fine-grained annotation benchmarking and whole-lesson evaluation, showing where AI systems can assist classroom video analysis and where expert judgment remains necessary across varied subjects, classroom formats, and annotation difficulty levels.

</details>


### [130] [Human-Alignment, Calibration, and Activation Patterns in Large Language Model Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30675)
*Kyle Moore,Jesse Roberts,Daryl Watson,William Ward,Grayson Heyboer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型的不确定性与人类不确定性的相似程度（即“不确定性对齐”），并分析指令微调对其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注大语言模型不确定性校准（判断准确性），但缺乏从人类视角探究模型不确定性是否像人类一样表达的问题。

Method: 通过在多项选择题和开放式事实回忆数据集上，同时考察大语言模型的外显行为和内部激活模式中是否存在类似人类的不确定性信号，并分析指令微调的影响。

Result: 发现模型同时表现出不确定性对齐和校准的证据，并在多种数据集上验证了这一现象。

Conclusion: 大语言模型的不确定性既具备校准能力，也在一定程度上与人类不确定性相似，指令微调会影响这两个方面。

Abstract: Uncertainty Quantification is a large and growing subfield of large language model behavioral analysis. Primarily to recognize and combat hallucination, the field has largely focused on measuring and improving calibration, the accuracy of uncertainty judgments to task efficacy. In this work, we investigate the relatively underexplored question of how similar large language model uncertainty is to human uncertainty. We investigate the presence and strength of human-similar uncertainty signals, deemed uncertainty alignment, in large language model overt behavior and internal activation patterns. We identify whether the models show evidence of simultaneous alignment and calibration on a variety of datasets covering both multiple choice and open ended factual recall. And we characterize the effect of instruct fine-tuning on each of these facets.

</details>


### [131] [ElasticMem: Latent Memory as a Learnable Resource for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30690)
*Tao Feng,Chongrui Ye,Tianyang Luo,Jingjun Xu,Xueqiang Xu,Haozhen Zhang,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ElasticMem提出了一种弹性潜记忆增强框架，通过自适应检索和可学习的变长记忆预算分配，显著提升LLM在长时交互中的推理能力，并在MemorySuite评测中超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强方法要么将记忆作为固定文本资源拼接进上下文导致高token开销和对噪声敏感，要么使用固定容量的潜空间记忆接口，造成查询依赖的记忆效用与固定分配之间的不匹配。

Method: ElasticMem构建离线潜记忆库（含检索键和内容缓存），从推理器的隐藏状态自适应检索记忆，通过学到的策略为每条检索到的记忆分配可变潜预算，并将选中的潜状态作为软记忆token注入生成过程，整个记忆使用过程通过GRPO进行优化。

Result: 在MemorySuite上，基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和7B-Instruct，QA加权平均准确率分别提升26.2%和24.6%，ALFWorld成功率提升66.3%和27.2%，同时token成本最低。

Conclusion: 自适应检索和弹性预算分配帮助ElasticMem优先选择有用证据和可迁移计划，超越了刚性的余弦相似度方法。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents to reason coherently across extended interactions, personalize responses, and reuse past experience. However, existing memory-augmented methods typically treat memory as a fixed resource: text-space approaches concatenate retrieved memories into the context window, causing substantial token overhead and sensitivity to noisy evidence, while latent-space approaches reduce textual cost but still rely on rigid retrieval or fixed-capacity memory interfaces. This creates a mismatch between query-dependent memory utility and fixed memory allocation. We propose ElasticMem, a memory-augmented LLM framework that learns to use memory as an elastic latent resource. ElasticMem builds an offline latent memory bank with retrieval keys and content caches, retrieves memories adaptively from the reasoner's hidden state, assigns each retrieved memory a variable latent budget through a learned policy, and injects selected latent states as soft memory tokens for generation. The full memory-use process is optimized with downstream task rewards through group-relative policy optimization. We evaluate ElasticMem on MemorySuite, covering memory-intensive QA and embodied agent control. Across Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbones, ElasticMem improves weighted average QA accuracy by 26.2% and 24.6%, and improves ALFWorld success rate by 66.3% and 27.2%, respectively, over the strongest baselines, while achieving the lowest ALFWorld token cost. Ablations and qualitative analyses further show that adaptive retrieval and elastic budget allocation help ElasticMem prioritize useful evidence and transferable plans beyond rigid cosine similarity. Our code for ElasticMem will be released at https://github.com/ulab-uiuc/ElasticMem.

</details>


### [132] [SAGE: A Novelty Gate for Efficient Memory Evolution in Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30711)
*Sijia Wang,Dhanajit Brahma,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SAGE，一种基于新颖性检测的自适应门控机制，用于智能体LLM的记忆写入控制，在保持记忆质量的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作聚焦于记忆检索和存储，缺乏对写入端（write-side）的原则性控制，LLM需频繁判断新事实是否应被添加、合并或忽略。

Method: 将记忆演化建模为新颖性检测问题，使用基于von Mises-Fisher的密度估计器对候选事实打分，并通过自适应阈值（跟踪记忆存储几何结构）将其路由为ADD、NOOP或送LLM合并。

Result: 在LoCoMo上，对7个开源模型平均token-F1优于Mem0；在GPT-4o-mini上，添加阶段API成本降低3.4倍、延迟降低2.5倍；作为A-Mem的即插即用门控，可跳过约16-18%的LLM调用。

Conclusion: 新颖性感知的写入控制是提升长期智能体记忆质量和系统效率的实用手段。

Abstract: Agentic LLMs must continuously decide whether newly extracted facts should be added, merged with existing memories, or ignored, yet prior work has focused more on retrieval and storage than on principled write-side control. We frame memory evolution as a novelty-detection problem and propose SAGE, a Spherical Adaptive Gate for memory Evolution that scores candidate facts with a von Mises-Fisher-based density estimator over memory embeddings and routes them with an adaptive threshold that tracks memory-store geometry. SAGE resolves clearly novel facts as ADD, clearly redundant facts as NOOP, and sends only uncertain cases to an LLM merge step, reducing expensive write-time reasoning. On LoCoMo, SAGE achieves the best average token-F1 against Mem0 on all seven open-weight backbone comparisons, while on GPT-4o-mini it reduces add-phase API cost by 3.4$\times$ and add-phase latency by 2.5$\times$ with only a small average judge-score gap. As a drop-in binary gate for A-Mem, SAGE skips roughly 16-18% of LLM calls across five models with minimal quality change on open-weight backbones. These results suggest that novelty-aware write control is a practical lever for improving both memory quality and system efficiency in long-term agentic memory.

</details>


### [133] [ExpGraph: Model-Agnostic Experience Learning with Graph-Structured Memory for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30712)
*Tao Feng,Chongrui Ye,Tianyang Luo,Jingjun Xu,Xueqiang Xu,Haozhen Zhang,Zhigang Hua,Yan Xie,Shuang Yang,Ge Liu,Jiaxuan You*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ExpGraph框架，通过构建自演化的经验图，让冻结的LLM执行器无需参数更新即可复用历史经验，显著提升多任务性能并减少交互步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体从零解决任务，无法复用成功策略或失败教训；微调方法不够灵活，难以适应更强的执行器出现。

Method: 将历史轨迹总结为可复用技能和失败教训，组织成自演化经验图节点，通过图扩散和效用感知排序检索经验；用强化学习训练轻量级检索副驾驶，根据有无经验检索的执行器性能反馈来优化。

Result: 在ExpSuite上，静态任务比最强基线提升12.2%（小执行器）和4.7%（大执行器），智能体环境中提升21.4%和12.7%，交互步数分别减少12.7%和21.6%。

Conclusion: 图结构经验、效用感知排序和自适应检索三者共同实现跨任务和跨模型的有效经验复用，且框架具有模型无关性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have shown strong capabilities in reasoning, tool use, and multi-step interaction, but they often solve tasks from scratch and fail to reuse successful strategies or failure lessons from prior experience. Fine-tuning on collected experience can improve reuse, but it is inflexible when stronger or more suitable executors emerge. We propose ExpGraph, a model-agnostic experience learning framework that enables frozen and replaceable LLM executors to improve through external experience reuse without parameter updates. ExpGraph summarizes historical trajectories into reusable skills and failure lessons, organizes them as nodes in a self-evolving experience graph, and retrieves useful experiences through graph diffusion and utility-aware ranking. A lightweight retrieval copilot is trained with reinforcement learning using feedback that compares executor performance with and without retrieved experiences, while the graph is updated online from downstream task outcomes. We evaluate ExpGraph on ExpSuite, covering question answering, mathematical reasoning, code generation, and multi-step agentic environments including ALFWorld and AppWorld. ExpGraph improves over the strongest baseline by 12.2% and 4.7% on static tasks with smaller and larger executors, and by 21.4% and 12.7% in agentic environments, while reducing average interaction steps by 12.7% and 21.6%. Ablations show that graph-structured experience, utility-aware ranking, and adaptive retrieval jointly enable effective experience reuse across diverse tasks and executor models.

</details>


### [134] [Neuron-Level Interventions for Gendered and Gender-Neutral Generation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30717)
*Zhiwen You,Nafiseh Nikeghbal,Jana Diesner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型中与性别相关的神经元（女性、男性和性别中立三类），提出神经元级干预方法，通过激活或屏蔽性别相关神经元实现对生成文本性别形式的精确控制，并发现这些神经元主要集中在模型的前几层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多从二元性别视角（女性 vs 男性）分析语言模型中的性别偏见，对性别中立形式（如they/them代词或中性职位名称）关注不足，且性别信号在模型内部表示中的编码方式尚不明确。

Method: 提出神经元级干预方法，识别与三类性别（女性、男性、性别中立）高度相关的神经元；通过控制生成实验，验证激活或屏蔽这些神经元可引导句子朝向目标性别形式同时保持原意；构建了两个包含三类性别标签的受控句子数据集，并通过人工评估验证数据质量。

Result: 性别特定神经元在模型各层中分布不均，主要集中在最早几层，后面层贡献较小；相比现有方法，该方法能实现更精确的性别控制，对非目标性别类别的泄漏更少，输出质量更稳定。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了语言模型中性别信息的编码方式，提供了一种简单有效的性别干预方法，可用于神经元干预评估和性别偏见缓解。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) can produce gendered language and stereotypes even when given neutral prompts. Most prior work on gender bias in LMs primarily examines gender through a binary lens (feminine vs. masculine), with limited attention to gender-neutral forms, such as they/them pronouns or neutrally phrased job titles. How gender-related signals are encoded in the internal representations of LMs remains an open question. In this work, we study gender-specific neurons in LMs across three categories: feminine, masculine, and gender-neutral. We propose a neuron-level intervention method to identify neurons that are strongly tied to each gender category. We then test these neurons through controlled generation, showing that activating or masking gender-related neurons can steer a sentence toward a target gender form while preserving its original meaning. To evaluate the effectiveness of our gender-intervention approach, we curate two datasets with controlled sentences labeled across all three gender categories and validate the data quality through human evaluation. Experiments on two open-source LMs show that gender-specific neurons are not evenly distributed across model layers; instead, they concentrate heavily in the earliest layers with smaller contributions from later layers. Compared to existing methods, our method achieves more precise gender control, with less leakage into non-target gender categories and stable output quality through two evaluation criteria. Overall, our work examines how gender is encoded in LMs and provides a simple yet effective approach toward controlled gender intervention for both neuron intervention evaluation and gender bias mitigation. Code and datasets are available at: https://github.com/zhiwenyou103/Gender-Neuron-Intervention

</details>


### [135] [Skill is Not One-Size-Fits-All: Model-Aware Skill Alignment for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30723)
*Jianxiang Yu,Jiapeng Zhu,Bochen Lin,Qier Cui,Zichen Ding,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现技能库的效果高度依赖模型本身，并提出MASA框架，通过两阶段流程（层次化技能进化 + 轻量级重写器）为不同模型自适应调整技能描述，显著提升性能且具备泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库被当作模型无关的，即不同能力和大小的模型使用相同的技能描述，但实验发现同一技能对不同模型的效果差异巨大，甚至可能产生负面影响。

Method: MASA包含两阶段：(1)层次化技能进化流程，利用爬山算法和UCB驱动的树搜索，结合环境反馈和模型能力画像，迭代重写通用和任务特定技能；(2)轻量级模型条件技能重写器，在进化轨迹上训练，实现单次前向传播即可适配。

Result: 在三个交互环境和四个模型骨干上，MASA一致取得最佳性能，比最强基线提升高达25.8个点。训练好的重写器能泛化到未见任务和环境，推理成本远低于更大的教师LLM。

Conclusion: 技能有效性是模型依赖的，MASA通过自适应技能对齐有效解决该问题，且训练出的轻量级重写器具备跨任务、跨环境的泛化能力。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly retrieve externally curated skills-procedural instructions retrieved at decision time-to improve performance on long-horizon interactive tasks. Existing skill libraries are typically treated as model-agnostic, reusing the same skill formulations across backbones with substantially different capacities and behaviors. However, our controlled experiments across multiple model scales show that skill effectiveness is strongly model-dependent: a skill that benefits one backbone can harm another. Motivated by this observation, we propose MASA Model-Aware Skill Alignment, a framework that adapts skills to each target backbone without modifying agent weights. MASA operates in two stages: (1) a hierarchical skill evolution pipeline that iteratively rewrites general and task-specific skills using hill climbing and UCB-driven tree search, guided by environment feedback and model capability profiles; and (2) a lightweight model-conditioned skill rewriter trained on evolution trajectories to reproduce the adaptation in a single forward pass. Experiments across three interactive environments and four backbones show that MASA consistently achieves the best overall performance, with gains of up to 25.8 points over the strongest baseline. The learned rewriter further generalizes to unseen tasks and environments without additional search, consistently outperforming a much larger teacher LLM at a fraction of the inference cost.

</details>


### [136] [MosaicLeaks:Privacy Risks in Querying-in-the-Open for Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30727)
*Alexander Gurung,Spandana Gella,Alexandre Drouin,Issam H. Laradji,Perouz Taslakian,Rafael Pardinas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度研究智能体在结合本地私密文档与外部工具时存在的隐私泄露风险，提出了MosaicLeaks基准（含1001个多跳任务），并设计了一种名为PA-DR的强化学习框架，在保持任务性能的同时显著降低隐私泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体在结合本地私密文档与外部检索工具时，外部查询可能泄露本地上下文中的敏感信息，且马赛克效应（单个查询看似无害但聚合后泄露隐私）放大了这一风险，现有方法缺乏对此系统性评估与防御。

Method: 1) 构建MosaicLeaks基准（1001个多跳任务，链接私密企业文档与公开网页）；2) 使用对抗LLM在三级别（研究意图、私密问题答案、可验证声明）评估泄露；3) 提出PA-DR（隐私感知深度研究）RL框架，结合任务成功的情境奖励与学习到的隐私分类器，对单查询和马赛克级泄露进行密集信用分配。

Result: 各模型在三个级别上均频繁泄露；零样本隐私提示减少但未消除泄露；仅优化任务性能的RL加剧泄露。PA-DR训练Qwen3-4B-Instruct将准确率从48.7%提升至58.7%，并将答案级和全信息泄露从34.0%降至9.9%。

Conclusion: PA-DR框架能有效平衡任务性能与隐私保护，显著降低深度研究智能体在多跳任务中的隐私泄露风险。

Abstract: Deep research agents increasingly combine private local documents with external tools like web retrieval, creating a privacy risk: an agent's external queries may leak sensitive information from its local context. This risk is amplified by the mosaic effect, where individual queries may appear harmless but become revealing in aggregate. We introduce MosaicLeaks, a benchmark of 1,001 multi-hop deep research tasks that chain private enterprise documents and a public web corpus, forcing agents to make external queries that depend on local information. We evaluate leakage with an adversary LLM that observes only the agent's external queries and attempts to infer private information at three levels: the agent's research intent, answers to specific private questions and verifiable claims about the enterprise documents. We find that models across families and sizes frequently leak at all three levels, that zero-shot privacy prompting reduces but does not eliminate leakage and that reinforcement learning for task performance alone worsens leakage. To address this, we propose Privacy-Aware Deep Research (PA-DR), an RL framework that combines situational rewards for task success with a learned privacy classifier to provide dense credit assignment over both per-query and mosaic-level leakage. Training Qwen3-4B-Instruct with PA-DR improves accuracy from 48.7% to 58.7% and reduces answer and full-information leakage from 34.0% to 9.9%.

</details>


### [137] [Efficient Diffusion LLMs via Temporal-Spatial Parallel Decoding and Confidence Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30753)
*Zekai Li,Ji Liu,Yiqing Huang,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于轨迹感知的扩散LLM解码框架（TSPD+CE），通过动态控制每个token的去噪收敛时机来减少冗余计算，从而加速并行文本生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散LLM推理延迟高，因为大量去噪步骤浪费在已确定token的冗余精炼和重复掩码上；现有加速方法依赖局部置信度启发式或固定调度，对提示和任务变化敏感，且忽略了序列内的位置效应。

Method: 将扩散解码视为动态控制问题，提出两个组件：1) TSPD（时空并行解码），使用轻量级控制器，利用每个token的轨迹特征（置信度、熵、动量）和位置信息决定何时收敛；2) CE（置信度外推），无训练的状态空间模块，预测未来logit趋势及不确定性，支持前瞻性决策（安全预判和振荡时的主动稳定）。

Result: TSPD和CE协同减少不必要的去噪迭代，同时保持输出质量，并能与KV缓存等系统优化无缝结合。

Conclusion: 通过token级动态控制（轨迹感知）而非固定调度，可显著加速扩散LLM推理，且方法具有任务鲁棒性和系统兼容性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based large language models (dLLMs) support parallel text generation via iterative denoising, yet inference remains latency-heavy because many steps are spent on redundant refinement and repeated remasking of tokens whose final values are already determined. Prior acceleration methods mainly depend on step-local confidence heuristics or fixed schedules, which are sensitive to prompt and task variation and ignore strong positional effects within a sequence. We cast diffusion decoding as a dynamic control problem and show that token-wise denoising trajectories provide the key signal for reliable control. We propose a trace-aware decoding framework with two components. First, Temporal-Spatial Parallel Decoding (TSPD) uses a lightweight temporalspatial controller that consumes per-token trajectory features, including confidence, entropy, and momentum, together with token position, to decide when a token has converged and can be safely fixed. Second, we introduce Confidence Extrapolation (CE), a training-free state-space module that forecasts future logit trends with uncertainty to support proactive decisions, including safe look-ahead and targeted stabilization when trajectories are oscillatory or underconfident. Together, TSPD and CE reduce unnecessary denoising iterations while preserving output quality, and they compose cleanly with system optimizations such as KV caching.

</details>


### [138] [Pairwise Reference Alignment as a Model-Level Ordinal Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30758)
*Mujing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了成对参考对齐（Pairwise Reference Alignment），将其建模为模型评分函数诱导的有序观测量，并提供了统计估计框架和初步实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作大量使用成对偏好数据，但对“模型能否区分偏好与非偏好回应”这一基础测量问题缺乏清晰的形式化定义。

Method: 定义对齐观测量为模型排序与参考偏好排序一致的概率，引入类似序参量的中心化统计量，并讨论基于间隔的扩展形式，给出有限样本估计量和集中界。

Result: 在Qwen2.5模型和RewardBench上，所提出的统计量随模型规模和指令微调而增加，且不同参考对子集上的变化符合理论预期。

Conclusion: 本文提供了成对参考对齐的概念与统计形式化框架，明确了参考对分布的作用，并将一般有序观测量与具体的评分选择区分开来。

Abstract: Pairwise preference data is widely used in language-model evaluation and alignment, often for model ranking, reward modeling, or preference optimization. This note formulates a more basic measurement question: given a reference distribution of pairwise preferences, what model-level quantity is estimated when we test whether a model ranks preferred responses above rejected responses?
  We define pairwise reference alignment as an ordinal observable induced by a model scoring function. Given a reference pair distribution $P_{\mathrm{pair}}$ over triples $(x,y^+,y^-)$, and a scalar model score $S_M(x,y)$, we define the alignment observable as the probability that the model-induced ordering agrees with the reference preference ordering. We further define a centered order-parameter-like statistic and discuss a margin-based extension. The resulting quantities admit simple finite-sample estimators and concentration bounds under independent sampling assumptions.
  This note does not introduce a new benchmark. It provides a conceptual and statistical formulation for pairwise reference alignment, clarifies the role of the reference pair distribution, and distinguishes the general ordinal observable from scoring choices such as normalized log-probability or energy-based scores. We also provide an initial empirical study on Qwen2.5 models and RewardBench, where the proposed statistics increase with model size and instruction tuning and vary across reference-pair subsets as predicted by the formulation.

</details>


### [139] [Eywa: Provenance-Grounded Long-Term Memory for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30771)
*Resham Joshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Eywa是一种以证据为先的、可溯源的内存架构，通过分离证据存储、事实提取、上下文检索与答案策略，在多个基准测试上达到80-90%+的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体记忆系统将源证据、提取事实、检索上下文和答案策略混为一谈，导致难以诊断错误根源（如遗漏证据、提取错误、状态过时、检索丢失或模型行为问题）。

Method: Eywa构建了基于证据先于信念的内存架构：先存储不可变的源证据，再从其中推导规范化事实；通过类型化信号和源支持验证提取的记忆；采用确定性多路径读路径（零LLM调用）检索有限上下文；检索结果与答案指令分离，支持不同答案模型的评估。

Result: 在LoCoMo C1-C4上达到90.19%准确率；LongMemEval-S上达到88.2%检索充分性准确率；BEAM基准上达到81.45%平均nugget分数和85.29% pass@score≥0.5。

Conclusion: Eywa通过证据溯源和确定性检索路径，有效分离了记忆系统的各环节，显著提升了跨会话AI智能体的记忆可靠性和可诊断性。

Abstract: AI agents that persist across sessions need memory they can retrieve, audit, update, and erase. Existing memory systems often collapse source evidence, extracted facts, retrieved context, and answer policy into one opaque prompt path, making failures difficult to diagnose: a wrong answer may come from missing evidence, unsupported extraction, stale state, retrieval loss, or answer-model behavior. We present Eywa, a provenance-grounded memory architecture built around evidence before belief. Eywa stores immutable source evidence before deriving canonical facts, validates extracted memories against typed signals and source support, and retrieves bounded memory context through a deterministic multi-route read path with zero LLM calls inside retrieval. Retrieved context is returned separately from answer instructions, allowing the same memory substrate to be evaluated across frontier, budget, and local answer models. Under a frozen, artifact-recorded retrieval configuration, Eywa reaches 90.19% judge accuracy on the LoCoMo C1-C4 split with Claude Sonnet 4.6 write and QA roles. On LongMemEval-S, it reaches 88.2% retrieval-sufficiency accuracy. On BEAM, a 700-question technical-memory stress benchmark, it reaches 81.45% mean nugget score and 85.29% pass@score >= 0.5. Full per-question artifacts, including questions, gold answers, model answers, retrieved context, and labels, are published at https://eywa.to/research.

</details>


### [140] [XLGoBench: Detecting cross-lingual skill gaps with algorithmic tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30788)
*Purvam Jain,Preethi Jyothi,Vihari Piratla,Suvrat Raju*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一套合成算法任务基准，用于检测大语言模型在多语言能力上的差距。该基准具有跨语言可比、可扩展、可量化、透明等特点，并通过实验揭示了多个先进模型存在持续的跨语言差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型在多语言能力评估上缺乏一个跨语言可比、可扩展且透明的基准，难以准确检测模型在不同语言间的性能差距。

Method: 设计了一套合成算法任务，通过简单模板生成任务，确保跨语言任务内容一致、复杂度可调节、正确性可客观衡量、翻译错误可审计。

Result: 实验表明，多个最先进的大语言模型在不同语言间存在持续的、显著的性能差距。

Conclusion: 该基准能够有效检测跨语言差距，且差异表现足以（但不必然）说明模型存在跨语言能力不均衡的问题。

Abstract: We introduce a set of synthetic algorithmic tasks to detect cross-lingual gaps in the abilities of large language models. Our benchmark is commensurate across languages, since it requires models to perform the same underlying task in different languages; scalable, since each task can be generated at varying levels of complexity allowing it to be adapted to models with different capabilities; quantifiable, since every task admits an objective notion of correctness; and transparent, since tasks are generated from simple templates that can be readily audited for translation errors. Because our benchmark focuses on algorithmic tasks, differential performance is a sufficient -- but not necessary -- indicator of cross-lingual gaps. Nevertheless, we show through extensive experiments that our benchmark exposes persistent cross-lingual gaps in multiple state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [141] [Anchoring LLM Gender Bias to Human Baselines: A Cross-Lingual Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30804)
*Jiwoo Choi,Seonwoo Ahn,Tongxin Zhang,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究审计了六种大语言模型在四种语言中的性别刻板印象，发现模型表现出的刻板印象范围比人类跨国差异范围宽约2.5倍，且效应会跨语言叠加。通过四模式框架（一致、抑制、重组、放大）分析24个（模型×语言）组合，发现翻译会改变与刻板印象相关的属性，表明不存在单一的消除偏见管道能均匀解决跨语言的偏见问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注LLM是否存在偏见，但较少关注其与目标用户人群的偏差程度。本文旨在探究不同语言环境下，LLM的性别归因偏离其部署人群的程度。

Method: 采用HEXACO-100人格量表，将六种模型（3个英语中心模型、3个东亚模型）锚定在覆盖48个国家的跨文化人类数据集上。在英、韩、中、日四种语言中进行提示，引入四模式框架（concordance, suppression, reorganization, amplification）分析24个（模型×语言）组合。

Result: 模型刻板印象范围比人类跨国差异范围宽约2.5倍；英语中心模型在韩语提示下达到本地基线的5倍；翻译不仅改变刻板印象强度，还改变了与之关联的属性，会在表面校准良好的情况下掩盖显著的内部重组。

Conclusion: 没有单一的消除偏见管道能均匀解决跨语言边界上的偏见问题，LLM的性别刻板印象具有语言依赖性和高度复杂性。

Abstract: We audit six large language models (LLMs) for gender stereotyping across English, Korean, Chinese, and Japanese. Three were developed primarily for English-language use (Claude, GPT, Gemini) and three for East Asian use (DeepSeek, Syn-Pro, HyperCLOVA X). We adopt the HEXACO-100 personality inventory and anchor each model against a cross-cultural human dataset spanning 48 countries to ask not whether LLMs are biased, but how far their gender attributions drift from the populations they are deployed among. Our findings show that their stereotyping spans a range roughly 2.5 times wider than the entire cross-country range found in humans, and the effect can compound across languages. One English-centric model, prompted in Korean, reached 5 times the local baseline, even when the prompt stated the candidate had already been hired, which often dampens human stereotyping. To characterize such behaviors without ranking them, we introduce a four-pattern framework -- concordance, suppression, reorganization, and amplification -- across 24 (model x language) cells. Item-level analysis reveals that translation does not just rescale stereotypes, but changes the attributes tied to it, hiding significant rearrangement under the surface while appearing well-calibrated. Our results ultimately suggest that no single debiasing pipeline is likely to address bias evenly across linguistic boundaries.

</details>


### [142] [Incremental BPE Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30813)
*Shenghu Jiang,Ruihao Gong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种增量式BPE分词算法，在最坏情况下每个输入字节的处理时间为O(log² t)，整体复杂度为O(n log² t)，相比Hugging Face提速约3倍，并支持流式输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的BPE分词在处理流式输入或需要增量分词时效率较低，现有实现缺乏对增量分词的良好支持，难以满足大语言模型流水线中对低延迟和流式处理的需求。

Method: 设计了一种增量式BPE分词算法，通过维护每个前缀的分词结果，实现了对输入流的高效处理。算法在最坏情况下每个字节的处理时间为O(log² t)，整体时间复杂度为O(n log² t)。同时提出了"急切输出"算法，在确定token边界后立即输出，支持流式输出。

Result: 相比Hugging Face的tokenizers加速约3倍，在病态输入上相比OpenAI的tiktoken显著降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 证明了BPE分词可以在强最坏情况保证下进行增量处理，并在现代大语言模型流水线中提供实际的延迟收益。

Abstract: We propose a novel algorithm for incremental Byte Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenization. The algorithm processes each input byte in worst-case $\mathcal{O}(\log^2 t)$ time, leading to an overall complexity of $\mathcal{O}(n \log^2 t)$, where $n$ is the input length and $t$ is the maximum token length. The algorithm incrementally maintains BPE tokenization results for every prefix of the input text, implementing the standard BPE merge procedure defined by a fixed set of merge rules. This enables efficient partial tokenization in streaming settings. Functioning as a drop-in replacement for standard BPE, our approach achieves a speedup of up to ${\sim}3\times$ over Hugging Face's tokenizers, and demonstrates significant latency reductions over OpenAI's tiktoken on pathological inputs. We further introduce an eager output algorithm that enables streaming output, emitting tokens as soon as token boundaries are determined during incremental tokenization. Overall, our results demonstrate that BPE tokenization can be performed incrementally with strong worst-case guarantees, while providing practical latency benefits in modern large language model pipelines. Code: https://github.com/ModelTC/mtc-inc-bpe

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Agreement: Scoring Panel-Surfaced Biomedical Entity Candidates for Curator Triage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30826)
*Shuheng Cao,Ruiqi Chen,Renjie Cao,Zhenhao Zhang,Siyu Zhang,Tingting Dan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BioConCal方法，通过多LLM面板一致性信号和监督评分器，将生物医学NER的候选实体验证AUROC从0.753提升至0.910，在0.95精确率目标下筛选出1340个候选（原始一致性仅293个），核心价值在于将噪声面板流转化为高收益审核队列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学NER看似简单，但LLM输出的"合理"提及并不等同于遵循语料库约定的正确结果；多LLM一致性只是显著性信号而非正确性信号，需要更可靠的候选验证机制。

Method: 构建8个LLM在5个公开数据集上的对齐候选主表，提出BioConCal域内监督评分器，在推理时利用无金标一致性、提及特征、表面可得性和文档特征对固定候选流进行评分。

Result: 域内AUROC从原始一致性的0.753提升至0.910；在验证集选择的0.95精确率阈值下，筛选出1340个候选（原始一致性仅293个），经验精确率0.939，候选级召回率0.592，语料级召回率0.523，面板内行标签上限为0.883。

Conclusion: 主要收益并非找回所有面板成员遗漏的实体，而是将噪声面板流重塑为高产出的审核队列；实体类型迁移时需要目标域验证，精确字符定位仍为独立的确定性后处理步骤。

Abstract: Biomedical NER is deceptively simple for modern LLMs: plausible biomedical mentions are easy to surface, but corpus-convention correctness depends on annotation conventions, span boundaries, entity granularity, and type schemas. Multi-LLM agreement is a salience signal, not corpus-convention correctness. We introduce a candidate-level panel-output benchmark for panel-surfaced candidate verification, where the unit is an aligned candidate surfaced by an explicitly defined multi-model panel rather than a standalone extractor output. The benchmark aligns eight LLMs' predictions over five public biomedical NER datasets into a candidate master table. BioConCal is an in-domain supervised scorer that instantiates this layer with inference-time gold-free agreement, mention, surface-availability, and document features for a fixed candidate stream. In domain, BioConCal improves AUROC from 0.753 for raw agreement to 0.910. At a validation-selected 0.95 precision target it selects 1,340 candidates at empirical test precision 0.939, compared with 293 for raw agreement. This corresponds to candidate-level recall 0.592 and corpus-level recall 0.523 against a within-panel row-label ceiling of 0.883. The main benefit is not recovering entities missed by every panel member, but reshaping a noisy panel stream into a higher-yield review queue. Under entity-type shift, thresholds require target-domain validation, and exact character localization remains a separate deterministic post-processing step.

</details>


### [144] [Your Teacher Can't Help You Here: Combating Supervision Fidelity Decay in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30833)
*Yanjiang Liu,Jie Lou,Xinyan Guan,Yuqiu Ji,Hongyu Lin,Ben He,Xianpei Han,Le Sun,Xing Yu,Yaojie Lu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lookahead Group Reward方法，解决on-policy蒸馏中因学生生成长序列导致的监督保真度衰减问题，在数学和代码基准测试上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy蒸馏中，学生模型生成的长序列会导致教师模型置信度下降，从而削弱蒸馏效果，这个问题被称为监督保真度衰减。

Method: 提出Lookahead Group Reward，通过评估学生候选token在下一步激发的教师置信度作为奖励，并采用熵触发的树注意力机制保持计算效率。

Result: 在6个数学和代码基准上，7B学生模型的mean@8提升2.57分，在AIME-26的长序列任务上提升4.92分。

Conclusion: Lookahead Group Reward有效缓解了长序列蒸馏中的监督保真度衰减，且随着生成长度增加，优势更加明显。

Abstract: On-policy distillation transfers reasoning capabilities by training a student model on its own generated trajectories using token-level feedback from a teacher. However, we identify a critical bottleneck, \textbf{Supervision Fidelity Decay (SFD)}: as student-generated prefixes lengthen, the teacher's next-token distribution becomes less confident and less discriminative. Consequently, the teacher-dependent corrective signal in reverse-KL distillation weakens, causing student drift to compound across long reasoning chains. To mitigate SFD, we introduce \textbf{Lookahead Group Reward (\ours{})}. Building on the insight that next-step teacher confidence reflects the discriminative strength of future reverse-KL supervision, \ours{} evaluates the student's top-K candidate tokens by the teacher confidence they induce at the subsequent step and assigns a group-normalized reward. To maintain computational efficiency, we further design an entropy-triggered tree-attention mechanism. Across six math and code benchmarks, \ours{} improves mean@8 by \textbf{2.57} points over OPD for a 7B student, with gains increasing in longer-generation and reaching +\textbf{4.92} points on AIME-26 at 39k tokens.

</details>


### [145] [Fine-Tuning Improves Information Conveyance in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30844)
*Yuwei Cheng,Weiyi Tian,Haifeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Canopy Entropy (CE*)，从树结构视角量化语言模型生成空间的多样性，发现微调并未简单减少不确定性，而是将其重组为更高效、更有语义信息的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为微调会降低LLM的不确定性和多样性，但忽略了输出长度这一关键混淆变量，无法捕捉整个生成过程的熵分布。

Method: 提出Canopy Entropy (CE*)，将语言生成视为树结构，"冠层"代表所有可能生成路径。CE*联合捕捉输出长度N和生成序列的熵，等于总香农熵H(N, Y_{1:N}|X)，并引入长度-熵率相关系数ρ(N, r_N)衡量信息传递效率。

Result: 微调模型在总熵下降的同时，持续表现出更强的正相关ρ(N, r_N)；在控制多种变量后，微调使熵率与语义多样性之间的相关性强度几乎翻了三倍。

Conclusion: 微调并非简单减少不确定性，而是从根本上重组不确定性，使其转化为更信息密集、语义更丰富的生成结果。

Abstract: Fine-tuning is often believed to reduce uncertainty and diversity in large language models, but existing analyses overlook output length, a key confounder, and therefore fail to capture how uncertainty is distributed across an entire generation rollout. To address this, we propose Canopy Entropy ($\mathrm{CE}^\star$), a measure that views language generation from a tree perspective, where ``canopy'' represents the space of all possible rollouts, making $\mathrm{CE}^\star$ naturally quantify the effective size of the generation space. $\mathrm{CE}^\star$ jointly captures uncertainty in both the output length $N$ and the generated sequence $Y_{1:N}$ -- indeed, we show that it equals to total Shannon entropy $H(N, Y_{1:N}\mid X)$, where $X$ denotes the prompt. This formulation yields interpretable metrics, including a length-entropy correlation term $ρ(N, r_N)$, where $r_N$ is the entropy rate, quantifying information conveyance efficiency by indicating whether longer outputs are more or less informative per token. Empirically, across tasks and model families, we find that fine-tuned models consistently exhibit stronger positive correlation $ρ(N, r_N)$, even when total entropy decreases. Furthermore, after controlling for model family, task, prompt, and output-length effects, we find that fine-tuning nearly triples the correlation strength between entropy rate and semantic diversity, suggesting that aligned models convert token uncertainty into semantic diversity more efficiently. Overall, these results demonstrate that fine-tuning does not simply reduce uncertainty, but fundamentally reorganizes it into more informative and semantically meaningful generations. Our code is available at https://github.com/WeiyiTian/canopy-entropy.

</details>


### [146] [Speculative Pipeline Decoding: Higher-Accruacy and Zero-Bubble Speculation via Pipeline Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30852)
*Yijiong Yu,Huazheng Wang,Shuai Yuan,Ruilong Ren,Ji Pei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPD通过流水线并行和投机模块，让LLM并行处理多个token，实现零延迟气泡的加速解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流投机解码方法依赖多token预测，存在预测难度递增和串行延迟问题，需要更高效的加速方案。

Method: 将目标LLM划分为n个流水线阶段，使其能并行处理n个token；同时引入投机模块，在不同流水线深度聚合中间特征来预测下一个token，与目标模型流水线步骤严格并行执行。

Result: 相比主流基线方法，SPD实现了显著更高的理论加速比，具有高度可扩展性。

Conclusion: SPD是一种高效、可扩展的LLM解码加速框架，通过流水线并行和并行投机预测，有效解决了传统方法的瓶颈。

Abstract: Speculative Decoding (SD) accelerates low-concurrency LLM inference by employing a draft-then-verify paradigm. However, mainstream methods typically rely on multi-token prediction, which introduces escalating prediction difficulty and serial drafting latency. To address these, we propose Speculative Pipeline Decoding (SPD), a groundbreaking framework that unlocks the true potential of pipeline parallelism. By partitioning the target LLM into $n$ pipeline stages, SPD allows LLM to process $n$ tokens in parallel to accelerate decoding. To continuous fill the pipeline in single sequence decoding, a speculation module aggregates intermediate features across different pipeline depths to predict the next token, executing strictly in parallel with the target model's pipeline step, to realize bounded difficulty, higher acceptance rates, and zero latency bubbles. Our experiments demonstrate that SPD achieves a significantly higher theoretical speedup compared to mainstream baselines, offering a highly scalable solution for LLM decoding acceleration. Our code is available at https://github.com/yuyijiong/speculative_pipeline_decoding

</details>


### [147] [MADS: Model-Aware Diverse Core Set Selection for Instruction Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30857)
*Yi Bai,Wenhao Zhang,Yao Chen,Jiao Xue,Zhumin Chen,Pengjie Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模型激活状态的核心集选择方法，通过神经激活特征确保指令微调数据的多样性，仅用15%数据即可提升大模型性能2.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核心集选择方法主要基于文本特征本身区分训练数据，与LLM自身对数据的理解和表示相脱离，难以确保所选核心集的多样性。

Method: 提出Model-Aware Diverse Core Set Selection方法，利用LLM推理时的神经激活状态来区分数据特征，基于模型内在激活特征实现覆盖性核心集选择。

Result: 在Alpaca-GPT4数据集上，用3B模型选择15%核心集，微调7B/8B/13B模型，在6个基准5项任务上平均提升2.5%。

Conclusion: 该方法能在减少数据需求的同时提升多下游任务性能，且小模型选择的核心集可有效用于大模型微调。

Abstract: Instruction fine-tuning is employed to enhance the instruction-following ability of large language models (LLMs). As the amount of instruction fine-tuning data increases, selecting the optimal core set becomes particularly important. However, ensuring the diversity of the core set remains a significant challenge. Existing methods predominantly distinguish different training data based on the text features themselves, decoupled from LLMs' own understanding and representation of the data. To address this issue, we propose a Model-Aware Diverse Core Set Selection method, which distinguishes data features based on the neural activation states during LLM inference. This approach serves as an efficient instantiation of coverage-based selection using model-intrinsic activation features to ensure the diversity in the core set. We extensively evaluate our method on six benchmarks that cover five distinct tasks. In our method, the core set selected by the 3B-parameter LLM performs effectively when utilized to fine-tune larger models with 7B, 8B, and 13B parameters. Experimental results on the Alpaca-GPT4 dataset, which comprises 52K instruction-response pairs, show that the core set, sized at 15\% of the original dataset and selected by Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct, achieves an average improvement of 2.5\% when fine-tuning four larger base models compared with training on the full dataset. The experimental results demonstrate that our method enhances model performance on multiple downstream tasks while reducing data requirements.

</details>


### [148] [dMoE: dLLMs with Learnable Block Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30876)
*Sicheng Feng,Zigeng Chen,Gongfan Fang,Xinyin Ma,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对扩散大语言模型（dLLM）与混合专家（MoE）架构结合时存在的块级并行解码与token级专家路由不匹配问题，本文提出dMoE框架，通过聚合块内token级专家分布为统一的块级专家分布来指导路由，大幅减少推理时激活的专家数量（从69.5降至14.6），在保留99.11%性能的同时减少76.64%-79.84%内存使用，并实现1.14×-1.66×端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）与混合专家（MoE）架构结合时，块级并行解码和token级独立专家路由存在根本性不匹配，导致推理时激活的独特专家数量剧增，使推理过程受限于内存带宽瓶颈。

Method: 提出dMoE，一种简单有效的块级MoE框架。核心思想是将每个块内所有token的专家分布聚合为统一的块级专家分布，以此更一致地指导专家路由，减少不必要的大量独特专家激活。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，dMoE将激活的独特专家数从69.5降至14.6，保留99.11%的原始性能，内存使用减少76.64%-79.84%，端到端延迟加速1.14×-1.66×。

Conclusion: dMoE有效解决了dLLM与MoE结合时的内存瓶颈问题，在几乎不损失性能的前提下显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models, offering competitive performance while naturally supporting parallel decoding. However, as dLLMs are increasingly integrated with Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures to scale model capacity, a fundamental mismatch arises between block parallel decoding and token-level expert selection. Specifically, each dLLM forward pass processes multiple tokens with bidirectional dependencies, whereas conventional MoE layers route each token independently. This mismatch substantially increases the number of uniquely activated experts, making inference increasingly memory-bound. To address this, we propose dMoE, a simple yet effective block-level MoE framework. The central idea of dMoE is to aggregate token-level expert distributions within each block into a unified block-level expert distribution, which is then used to guide expert routing in a more coherent manner. In this way, dMoE substantially reduces the number of uniquely activated experts during inference without sacrificing performance, thereby mitigating the memory-bound bottleneck. Extensive experiments across a variety of benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of dMoE. On average, dMoE reduces the number of uniquely activated experts from 69.5 to 14.6 while retaining 99.11% of the original performance. Meanwhile, it reduces memory usage by 76.64% to 79.84% and achieves 1.14$\times$ to 1.66$\times$ end-to-end latency speedup. Code is available at: https://github.com/fscdc/dMoE

</details>


### [149] [PatchWorld: Gradient-Free Optimization of Executable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30880)
*Jiaxin Bai,Yue Guo,Yifei Dong,Jiaxuan Xiong,Tianshi Zheng,Yixia Li,Tianqing Fang,Yufei Li,Yisen Gao,Haoyu Huang,Zhongwei Xie,Hong Ting Tsang,Zihao Wang,Lihui Liu,Jeff Pan,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PatchWorld是一个无需梯度的方法，通过反例引导的代码修复将离线轨迹转换为可执行的Python世界模型，在AgentGym环境中实现了76.4%的宏观成功率，并揭示了可执行世界模型中观测保真度与动作判别动力学之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本智能体环境通常建模为POMDP，但很少有工作研究是否可以通过可执行代码来构建世界模型，用于部分可观测条件下的预测和规划。

Method: 提出PatchWorld框架，通过反例引导的代码修复（counterexample-guided code repair）将离线轨迹转化为可执行的Python世界模型。该方法诱导符号化的信念状态程序，其动作更新可被检查、重放和局部修补，无需在预测模块中调用LLM。

Result: 在七个AgentGym环境中，PatchWorld-Simple达到76.4%的宏观成功率，在基于代码的规划评分中最高。同时发现人工指定的残差记忆偏差可改善观测保真度但会削弱决策效用。

Conclusion: 可执行世界模型中存在观测保真度与动作判别动力学之间的权衡：提升观测保真度可能以牺牲动作判别性为代价，反之亦然。

Abstract: Text-agent environments are typically modeled as partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs), assuming that the simulator's latent state and transition dynamics are hidden from the agent. Yet little work has examined whether executable code can be induced to serve as a world model for prediction and planning under partial observability. We introduce PatchWorld, a gradient-free framework that turns offline trajectories into executable Python world models through counterexample-guided code repair. Instead of predicting the next observation with a black-box model, PatchWorld induces symbolic belief-state programs whose action updates can be inspected, replayed, and locally patched. Across seven AgentGym environments, PatchWorld-Simple achieves the highest code-based planning score among evaluated methods, reaching 76.4\% macro success in live one-step lookahead while invoking no LLM calls inside the world-model prediction module itself. We further find that a human-specified residual-memory bias improves surface observation fidelity but weakens decision utility. This exposes a tradeoff in executable world models, since improving observation fidelity can come at the expense of action-discriminative dynamics, and vice versa. Code is available at https://github.com/HKBU-KnowComp/PatchWorld.

</details>


### [150] [EMBGuard: Constructing Hazard-Aware Guardrails for Safe Planning in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30924)
*Dongwook Choi,Taeyoon Kwon,Bogyung Jeong,Minju Kim,Yeonjun Hwang,Hyojun Kim,Byungchul Kim,Young Kyun Jang,Jinyoung Yeo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMBGuard是一种基于MLLM的具身智能体安全护栏，通过解耦物理风险推理与智能体策略，评估(视觉观察, 动作)对来识别危险配置并提供自然语言解释。同时构建了包含15.1K动作条件对的EMBHazard数据集和329个真实场景的EMBGuardTest基准，在2B/4B参数规模下达到与GPT-5.1等专有模型竞争的性能，并显著降低误报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能体在真实环境中部署时缺乏明确识别物理危害和进行动作条件风险推理的机制，导致要么错过危险交互，要么过度识别风险。

Method: 提出EMBGuard，基于MLLM的安全护栏，通过解耦物理风险推理与智能体策略，评估(视觉观察, 动作)对来识别危险并提供自然语言解释。同时构建训练数据集EMBHazard（15.1K动作条件对）和基准测试EMBGuardTest（329个真实场景，7类物理风险），通过危险与动作的组合变化生成多样场景。

Result: EMBGuard在2B和4B参数规模下取得与GPT-5.1、Gemini-2.5-Pro等专有MLLM竞争的性能，同时显著降低误报率，有利于实时部署。

Conclusion: EMBGuard有效解决了具身智能体安全推理的痛点，以小规模模型实现高效的风险识别，代码、数据和模型已开源。

Abstract: MLLM-powered embodied agents deployed in real-world environments encounter physical hazards. However, existing approaches lack explicit mechanisms for identifying hazards and reasoning about action-conditioned risks, leading agents to either miss risky interactions or over-identify risks. To address this, we propose EMBGuard, the first MLLM-based safety guardrail for embodied agents designed to decouple physical risk reasoning from agent policy. By evaluating a (visual observation, action) pair, EMBGuard identifies hazardous configurations and provides natural language explanations of potential risks. Alongside EMBGuard, we contribute EMBHazard, a training dataset of 15.1K action-conditioned pairs, and EMBGuardTest, a benchmark of 329 manually curated real-world scenarios spanning seven physical risk categories. Through compositional variation of hazards and actions, we generate diverse risky and benign scenarios that agents may encounter during planning. Despite its compact size (2B, 4B), EMBGuard achieves performance competitive with proprietary MLLMs (e.g., GPT-5.1, Gemini-2.5-Pro) while significantly reducing the false-positive rates that hinder real-time deployment. We make the code, data, and models publicly available at https://github.com/dongwxxkchoi/EMBGuard

</details>


### [151] [Do Large Language Models Encode Institutional Experience? Evidence from Cross-Linguistic Moral Reasoning Under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30934)
*Nattavudh Powdthavee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在不同语言中表现出系统性道德推理差异，这种差异源于语言编码了特定制度环境的道德先验。在明确制度框架下无显著差异，但在制度模糊场景中，跨语言道德分歧增加且与真实制度差异相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM跨语言道德推理差异的根源，检验语言是否编码了制度环境中的道德先验，从而影响LLM的道德判断。

Method: 在9种语言、6个前沿LLM中开展两项预注册研究：研究1采用明确的制度框架（结果无效）；研究2引入制度模糊场景（发现显著跨语言差异）。

Result: 明确制度框架下无跨语言道德分歧；制度模糊场景下跨语言道德分歧增加，且与真实世界制度差异相关。明确框架会削弱这些效应。

Conclusion: 制度经验可能在语言中留下可检测的痕迹，影响LLM的道德推理；但明确的制度提示会抑制这些差异的表达。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic differences in moral reasoning across languages, yet the source of this variation remains unclear. We test the hypothesis that languages encode aspects of the institutional environments in which they are spoken, allowing LLMs to inherit institution-specific moral priors through training. Across nine languages spanning a broad gradient of institutional quality, six frontier LLMs, and two preregistered studies, we examine moral dilemmas whose acceptability depends on institutional functioning. In Study 1, explicit institutional framing produced uniformly null results: cross-linguistic moral divergence did not increase in institutionally contingent scenarios, nor did it track institutional differences between language communities. In Study 2, we introduced institutionally ambiguous scenarios in which institutional stakes were present but not explicitly stated. Under these conditions, cross-linguistic moral divergence increased relative to institutionally inert controls and, with one theoretically informative exception, was associated with real-world institutional differences between language communities. Explicit framing again attenuated these effects. These findings suggest that institutional experience may leave detectable traces in language that shape LLM moral reasoning, while also indicating that explicit institutional cues can suppress the expression of those differences.

</details>


### [152] [Extending AI for Research to the Humanities: A Multi-Agent Framework for Evidence-Grounded Scholarship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30947)
*Yating Pan,Jiajun Zhang,Jun Wang,Qi Su*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SPIRE是一个面向人文学科的多智能体框架，通过 scholarly primitives 理论和多尺度细读机制实现基于证据的推理，在古典文献基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM研究Agent主要面向科学工程领域，优化于执行和检索，缺乏人文学科所需的基于证据的阐释性推理能力。人文学术要求忠实引述、可验证出处和细读，现有工具无法满足这一需求。

Method: SPIRE基于Scholarly Primitives理论，将人文学科常见操作建模为协作的智能体角色（来源发现、证据注释、比较、出处检查、采样、引用绑定和论证综合），并在多尺度细读基底（段落、上下文内图社区、跨上下文语义聚类）上运行。

Result: 在古典中文和希腊罗马拉丁文献的同行评审论文基准上，SPIRE在恢复引用的原始证据方面优于Naive LLM、Text RAG和GraphRAG，并在答案准确性、深度、覆盖范围和证据质量方面获得更高的盲审评分。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明，学术操作智能体和细读检索都对基于证据的论文生成有贡献。SPIRE有效弥补了当前研究Agent在人文学科证据推理方面的空白。

Abstract: LLM-based research agents have advanced rapidly in science and engineering, where research is organized around executable experiments, code, and quantitative signals.
